All 1456 ticks match on the fields above and `inv` (the regression runner's `bowgun_118`). `run_102.txt` is `door_101_102.txt` and a random walk with runs through room 102 from tick 3410 to 5400: record it with `--no-enemies --no-enemies-room 0 --ticks 5400`, and run arklay as `door_101_102.txt` does with `run_102_arklay.txt` over 4620 ticks, comparing at `--offset 780`. `stairs_118.txt` and `stairs_118_run.txt` want `--warp 118:-9900:0:-5935:1024` or `118:-20776:0:-9255:1024` (the two stairs) and `118:-9900:0:-4500:1024` for the run, `stairs_103.txt` wants `--warp 103:-21400:-5400:-13317:0` or `103:16900:-5400:-13722:0`, and `stairs_200.txt` wants `--warp 103:10303:-5400:-7823:0`, so that room 200 is entered through its real door (a `--warp 200` starts its entry scene at once). Record them over 1400 ticks (`stairs_118.txt`), 900 (`stairs_118_run.txt`), 700 (`stairs_103.txt`) and 2000 (`stairs_200.txt`). Compare each at `--offset 344` against an arklay `--room` of the warp's room with a setup script as above. Every field matches on every tick (in `stairs_200.txt` but for `rng` on the door's tick). The setup script's camera is the cut of the original's tick 343, the last before play, not of tick 344: from `--warp 103:-21400:-5400:-13317:0` the original is on cut 0 at tick 343 and cuts to 1 on tick 344. A setup on cut 1 puts arklay a cut ahead (2) on tick 344, and room 103's script, which hides or shows zombie slot 0 by the cut, then starts the zombies and `Rnd` elsewhere. Its other warps start on the same cut at both ticks.

`route_100_200.txt` is the route in one run, from the new game through every real door: 100, 101, 102, 118, 119, 11A, 11B, 103 and 200, up and down room 118's stairs and onto its bin, with no warp. Record it with `record --sound --no-enemies --keep-enemy-in 101:0 --flag 4:135 --input oracle/inputs-re2/route_100_200.txt --ticks 7400` (with `--shot-dir` and `--shot-every 1` for the frames). `--keep-enemy-in ROOM:SLOT` keeps that slot in that room only, here Kendo; `--flag 4:135` opens room 102's gate, which otherwise opens only when its zombies walk into enemy areas 4 and 5. On arklay's side run `--ui game --character 0 --sound --ticks 7400 --setup-script oracle/inputs-re2/route_100_200.lua --input oracle/inputs-re2/route_100_200.txt`, with no `--room`, and compare at offset 0 with `--fields room,cam,rseed,p.x,p.y,p.z,p.angle,p.beh,p.as,p.hp,mot,pneck,inv,snd,bgm,xavol,em` (the `3:` entries of `snd` dropped on both sides). The setup switches the same enemies off with `api:set_re2_no_enemies` and raises the flag. Both games switch rooms on the same ticks, 825 (101), 3087 (102), 3878 (118), 5011 (119), 5361 (11A), 5678 (11B), 6103 (103) and 6620 (200). Every field matches on every tick of every room, from tick 0, but `rseed` and `rng` on each door's own tick (790, 3052, 3843, 4976, 5326, 5643, 6068 and 6585, as in `door_101_102.txt`). That includes the door's second sound on every door, room 118's stair steps and the head's place (`pneck`) on the new game's first ticks (see `docs/internals/deviations/re2.md`, "Doors", "Footsteps" and "Player"). A new game's start has no `mot` gap: the one at room 101's load in `door_101_102.txt` comes from its `--room` start. Frame for frame (the original's 640 by 480 picture scaled to arklay's 320 by 240, the mean absolute difference per channel out of 255), the rooms' means are 0.50 (100), 0.62 (101), 1.09 (102), 0.25 (118), 0.50 (119), 0.88 (11A), 0.67 (11B), 0.34 (103) and 1.08 (200). Apart from the new game's first ticks (23.7 on tick 2, as the picture fades in), no frame is worse than 4.8, room 200's first after its door. The regression runner's `route_100_200` replays it against the stored recording, with these differences in its `ignore` list. Its `room = "new"` starts arklay's new game rather than a `--room`. The same input recorded with the enemies on (`record --sound --flag 4:135 --input oracle/inputs-re2/route_100_200.txt --ticks 7400`; on arklay's side `--setup-script oracle/inputs-re2/route_100_200_enemies.lua`, which only raises the flag) dies in room 100 in both games, and the game over returns to the title after tick 1459. It matches the original on every tick, the game over's music included (`docs/internals/deviations/re2.md`, "The route with the enemies on"). The runner's `route_100_200_enemies` compares those 1460 ticks.

`route_100_200_fight.txt` is the route with the enemies on, played as a player would: it shoots room 100's and room 102's zombies, steps past the ones on the floor, aims down at room 11A's crawler and runs past room 11B's and room 103's, and Leon reaches room 200 with full health. It raises no flags, so room 102's gate opens as its zombies walk into its enemy areas. Record it with `record --sound --input oracle/inputs-re2/route_100_200_fight.txt --ticks 9600` (with `--shot-dir` and `--shot-every 1` for the frames), and on arklay's side run `--ui game --character 0 --sound --ticks 9600 --setup-script oracle/inputs-re2/route_100_200_fight.lua --input oracle/inputs-re2/route_100_200_fight.txt`. 11A's and 11B's entry messages wait for a confirm press, which the input gives before its first move in each. Both games switch rooms on the same ticks: 391 (101), 2662 (102), 5490 (118), 6583 (119), 6935 (11A), 7989 (11B), 8306 (103) and 8819 (200). With the fields of `route_100_200` plus `pneck`, every tick matches but `rng` and `rseed` on each door's tick (356, 2627, 5455, 6548, 6900, 7954, 8271 and 8784); the head (`pneck`) matches on every tick, the first ticks after each get-item window closes included (`docs/internals/deviations/re2.md`, "The head after the get-item window"). Every zombie (`zm`, but for the speeds of slots not yet moved) matches too. The rooms' frame means are 0.77 (100), 0.62 (101), 0.65 (102), 0.29 (118), 0.99 (119), 0.76 (11A), 0.96 (11B), 0.46 (103) and 1.02 (200), 0.66 over the run. In room 102 the camera goes from cut 4 to 5 on tick 4435 and back on 4437, and both games keep frame 4434 on screen through 4438 (`docs/internals/deviations/re2.md`, "The frames of a cut"). No frame after the first ticks is worse than 7.4 (room 102's 3653). The regression runner's `route_100_200_fight` replays it, with those differences in its `ignore` list.

`route_claire_a_fight.txt` is Claire A's route with the enemies on, played as a player would, from her new game to room 200 through the same rooms and doors as Leon's: Claire's room scripts on the route are Leon's but for room 101 (Kendo's scene, and the bowgun with 15 bolts where Leon's has the shotgun) and room 200 (its entry scene's flag and its item). She starts with the 13-round handgun (0x03), fires twice in room 100, takes room 102's 30 rounds and shoots its four zombies, knocks room 118's west zombie down from the bin and runs past it (room 118's 15 rounds are only there on Hard, `ck 0, 25`), and from room 119 on plays Leon's fought route, taking room 11A's rounds; she reaches room 200 with full health. It raises no flags. Record it with `record --start claire --sound --input oracle/inputs-re2/route_claire_a_fight.txt --ticks 9720` (with `--shot-dir` and `--shot-every 1` for the frames), and on arklay's side run `--ui game --character 1 --sound --ticks 9720 --setup-script oracle/inputs-re2/route_claire_a_fight.lua --input oracle/inputs-re2/route_claire_a_fight.txt`. Room 102's zombies 2 and 3 start with the `Dest` the original's new works hold, from the memory arklay follows from the new game (`docs/internals/deviations/re2.md`, "What a zombie's slot held before"). Both games switch rooms on the same ticks: 391 (101), 2662 (102), 5562 (118), 6691 (119), 7043 (11A), 8097 (11B), 8414 (103) and 8927 (200). With the fields of `route_100_200_fight` plus the six zombie slots, every tick matches but `rng` and `rseed` on each door's tick (356, 2627, 5527, 6656, 7008, 8062, 8379 and 8892), the head included on every tick. Kendo's scene in room 101 has voice lines, so its ticks could depend on the recording's pace ("On Windows"), but a recording without frames, at about 30 ticks a second, and the stored one with every frame give the same trace on every compared field. The rooms' frame means are 0.88 (100), 0.62 (101), 0.55 (102), 0.29 (118), 0.90 (119), 0.56 (11A), 0.95 (11B), 0.46 (103) and 1.00 (200), 0.61 over the run. The worst frames are room 100's 97 (45.9) and room 102's 3212 (18.2), where the native recording shows the old cut a tick longer than the game's two-frame hold, after a slow tick (`docs/internals/deviations/re2.md`, "The frames of a cut"); no other frame after the new game's first ticks is worse than 5.2 (room 119's 6909). The regression runner's `route_claire_a_fight` replays it.

`route_claire_a_card.txt` carries Claire's route on from `route_claire_a_fight.txt`'s end (its first 9720 ticks are that input's) to the Blue Card Key's use at room 200's computer, the way #470 does for Leon. From the hall's entrance Claire takes the stairs down (a confirm at the railing), walks along the west side to door 2 and goes into room 202, where Marvin's scene plays with her own voiced lines and gives her the card: it needs no presses, and the scene ends with the script walking her out through door 20 to the hall's upper floor, where the hall's event plays. The input then waits until 13600, since the scene's length follows its voice lines: they hold fewer ticks in a recording with every frame than in one without, so Claire is free on 13505 without frames and earlier with them, and the input has to wait for the later. From 13600 she goes up the east walkway, through the gap in the counter's south wall (between `x` -13000 and -9816) into the computer's area, and checks the computer; its question's yes and a press every 30 ticks take it through the console to the card's use ("Room 200's computer"). Record it with `record --start claire --sound --input oracle/inputs-re2/route_claire_a_card.txt --ticks 14800` (with `--shot-dir` and `--shot-every 1`); arklay runs with `--voice-ticks` from the recording (the runner's `voice_from_ref`), `--character 1` and `route_claire_a_card.lua`, which is `route_claire_a_fight.lua`. In the recording with every frame the rooms switch on the fought route's ticks, then 10121 (202) and 12971 (200), the computer starts on 13953, takes the card on 14192 and hands back on 14625. With the fields of `route_claire_a_fight` plus `fgcom` and `pc`, every tick matches but `rng` and `rseed` on each door's tick (the fought route's eight, 10086 into 202 and 12936, the scripts' door back to the hall). The run compares every frame, 0 to 14799, with a mean of 0.70: 0.74 up to the computer (0.70 in room 200 before 202, 1.30 in 202, 0.79 back in the hall), 0.01 for the computer from 13953 to its hand back (its console, frame and cursor, #502 and #552), and 0.18 in the hall after it. The regression runner's `route_claire_a_card` replays it.

`route_200_computer.txt` carries Leon's route on from the fought route's end, and its first 9600 ticks are `route_100_200_fight.txt`'s. From the main hall (200) it goes through the door into room 202 on tick 10012. Marvin's scene there gives the Blue Card Key and walks Leon back out by itself (a door the scripts open, on tick 12783 in the reference). Then come room 200's event 5, the walk round the counter and up the east ramp to the computer, the control monitor's message, and "Will you use the computer?", which the input answers Yes on tick 13800. The reference (`--sound`, every frame, 13900 ticks) took 93 minutes to record. The regression runner's `route_200_computer` hands arklay the reference's voice lengths (`voice_from_ref`). From tick 13802 the original runs the computer (game mode 7, its console on cut 15; "Room 200's computer" above), and the run matches on every one of its 13900 ticks, apart from `Rnd` and `rseed` on each door's tick, with a frame mean of 0.76 over all its frames, the computer's first ticks included. Room 202's items aren't on the route: the scene leaves the room before Leon can move.

Leon A past the card, from room 200's computer to the darkroom (208), is checked room by room from warps first (`oracle/re2-regress.d/route_leon_a_208.toml`, #536). `leon_a_200_out` (`record --no-enemies --warp 200:-14936:-1800:1202:1992:6 --flag 4:6 --flag 4:7 --flag 4:102 --flag 4:70 --life 200 --give 0:2:13 --give 2:0x14:9`, `leon_a_200_out.txt`) starts at the computer once the card is used. Leon turns east along the upper floor, runs south to the counter, takes its 30 handgun rounds (the window opens on 497), runs east and down the east ramp, then west along the ground floor through cuts 3, 1 and 2, and presses door 1 on 1204, which loads room 201 on 1239. It matches on every tick but the door's (`Rnd` and `rseed`), with no frames: a warp into 200 starts on a scrolled cut. `leon_a_201` (`record --warp 201:-15773:0:-21630:2040:0 --life 200 --give 0:2:13 --give 2:0x14:39`, `leon_a_201.txt`) starts on that door's arrival with the licker on. Leon runs west round the north end of the bar along x ≈ -23000 (cuts 1 and 2), then south into cut 4 on 458, which starts the licker behind the window, and presses door 1 on 468, which loads room 204 on 503. It matches to 593, the door's tick apart, on the trace and `zm0` and `zp`, with a frame mean of 0.20 over 380 to 502. `leon_a_204_entry` (`oracle/re2-regress.d/leon_a_204.toml`, the same `record` line, `leon_a_204_entry.txt`) carries `leon_a_201`'s input on through room 204: door 1's arrival and `main_06`'s move into the corridor, the walk to the zombie's body, its two checks (a room message each), the crouch that takes its 15 handgun rounds through the get-item window, and the walk north that stops just short of area 7, where the window licker's scene (`main_09`) starts. It matches on every tick to 1699 but the 201 door's `Rnd` and `rseed`, on the trace, `zm0`, `zm1` and `zp` included. Two fixes got it there: the body's `Parts_num` counts its model's 17 parts, not its skeleton's 15 joints, and the licker behind 204's window draws its `Rnd` in its before-draw (`Em22_before`) on the get-item window's held frames, as `Game_loop` does (docs/internals/deviations/re2.md, "The get-item window's frames"). Its frame mean over 380 to 1699 is 0.83, and 0.61 with the message box blanked; the body's messages are a room's, which arklay draws where `Mess_set` puts them (`0xB90022`, #551), and door 1's model frames are about 1.3. `leon_a_204` (`leon_a_204.txt`, 2600 ticks) carries the same input on. Leon walks north into area 7, and the window scene (`main_09`, with film 10 shown as the oracle's films-off build shows it) holds him until 1790. He turns east and runs to the east wall (x -14273), so that the licker's knock-backs throw him south clear of the body's check (area 6, x -16484 to -15084): with fire as the action key, a shot from inside it would open the body's message. Then he turns north and fires aimed down every 12 ticks; level shots mostly miss a crawling licker. Its life is 115, and it dies at 2130 after ten hits; its two hits (1947 and 2014) leave Leon at 156. The warps of `leon_a_205`, `leon_a_207` and the two room 208 runs give him 60, which `route_200_208` (below) doesn't: at 156 he turns and walks at Fine's pace. He runs north and along the north wall to door 1, pressed on 2296, and room 205 loads on 2340. It matches on every tick to 2599 but the two doors' `Rnd` and `rseed` (468 and 2296), with a frame mean of 0.54 over 1700 to 2599. The fix that got it there: the turn on the spot makes a step's noise on its footfalls, which the listening licker hears (docs/internals/deviations/re2.md, "The turn's noise"). `leon_a_205` (`oracle/re2-regress.d/leon_a_205.toml`, `record --warp 205:-25670:0:-25775:3072:0 --life 60 --give 0:2:3 --give 2:0x14:54`, `leon_a_205.txt`) starts at door 0's arrival as `leon_a_204` leaves Leon, but with 60 life. 4:48 is clear, so the bodies in the walls stay frozen. Leon runs north up the west corridor, east along its north wall, and south-east from its east end, sliding along the walls to door 2. He presses action there on 730, outside the check areas 9 and 10, and room 207 loads on 765. It matches on every tick but door 2's `Rnd` and `rseed` (730) and the neck point's first 11 ticks (344 to 354): the original's neck starts where the warp's load left the last pose, and the two meet by 355. The frame mean over 360 to 899 is 0.44. `leon_a_207` (`oracle/re2-regress.d/leon_a_207.toml`, `record --warp 207:-25640:0:-16590:0:1 --life 60 --give 0:2:3 --give 2:0x14:54`, `leon_a_207.txt`, 2100 ticks) starts at that door's arrival with `leon_a_205`'s life and rounds and the room's four zombies on. Leon opens the status screen on 352 and COMBINEs the rounds onto the handgun (18 in, 39 left); play comes back on 647. Auto-aim takes the nearest body in sight, and a raised gun stays on its target, so he lowers and raises it for each: he turns north and steps up to kill slot 3 (55 life, four hits, dead on 785), steps back south, where slot 0 is in sight, and puts it down at 1 life (907), puts slot 2 down at 39 as it comes along the corridor (1057), kills slot 0 as it gets up (1231) and slot 2 as it gets up (four hits, the last as it falls: a zombie at 0 life or less that isn't hit again can get up at 1), fires on empty to reload from the 39 (1387), walks north to the corridor's west end, where slot 1 sees him, and kills it in two raises (dead on 1753). No zombie grabs him. He runs east to door 2, presses it on 1971, and room 208 loads on 2006 with 60 life, 10 rounds in the handgun and 21 spare. It matches on every tick but door 2's `Rnd` and `rseed` and the neck's first 11 ticks, the zombies, `zd`, the music and the sounds included. The fix that got it there: the status screen's open and close run the stopped enemies' `Root_ck` on the original's four ticks (docs/internals/deviations/re2.md, "The inventory"); without it slot 2 took the player as its `Dest` three ticks early on 801. Its frame mean over 650 to 1971 is 0.95: the zombies close to cut 1's camera, their shading and their blood's place (docs/internals/deviations/re2.md, "Zombies"). Room 208, the darkroom, has two runs (`oracle/re2-regress.d/leon_a_208.toml`), each with the carried life and rounds: `leon_a_208` (`record --warp 208:-15462:0:-15336:1029:0 --life 60 --give 0:2:10 --give 2:0x14:21`, `leon_a_208.txt`, 900 ticks) from door 2's arrival walks south, west along the counter and north to the ink ribbons' table, presses on 486, answers Yes on 642 (`1e:3` into slot 3 on 662, object 2 hidden) and closes the message on 722. It matches on every tick but the neck's first 12, `msg`, `hk`, `fgitem`, the save room's music (`ff0c`) and the footsteps included, with a frame mean of 0.47 over 360 to 899. `leon_a_208_entry` (`record --no-enemies --warp 207:-15328:0:-12706:990 --life 60 --give 0:2:10 --give 2:0x14:21`, `leon_a_208_entry.txt`, 1250 ticks) starts where `leon_a_207` leaves Leon at door 2, takes a step forward (from the warp the neck stays a unit off the original's until Leon moves) and opens the door on 356. Room 208 loads on 391; main 07 fades out (`ff07,0000` on 479, gone on 480), main 0C is in on 481 and plays on 482, the close cue sounds on 694, and play comes back on 698, 307 ticks after the load. Then it walks to the ribbons as `leon_a_208` does, 350 ticks later. It matches on every tick but the door's `Rnd` and `rseed`, the neck's first 12 ticks and `hk` over the load (391 to 481: the original lists room 207's objects until 208's are set up 90 ticks into the load, where arklay lists 208's from the load, while nothing is drawn). Its frames are judged from the door's press: 0.93 over 356 to 699 (the fade, then the door's model through 208's load at 0.9 to 1.3 a frame) and 0.47 over 700 to 1249.

`route_200_208` (`oracle/re2-regress.d/route_200_208.toml`, `route_200_208.txt`, 20620 ticks, `--sound` and every frame, recorded in 81 minutes) is the route from the new game to the ink ribbons in one run, with no warp. Its first 13802 ticks are `route_200_computer.txt`'s. The card's use goes on with `computer_200_card.txt`'s presses 13400 ticks later (that run's Yes, 400, is the route's 13800), and play comes back on 14475. Then each room run's input is joined on so that its tick 343, the warp's hand back, falls on the route's hand back after the door before: `leon_a_200_out.txt` 14132 ticks later and `leon_a_204.txt` (201 and 204) 15359 later, both unchanged. Room 200's door 1 hands back 366 ticks after its press (15336 to 15702) where the other doors take 340, in the original too. The licker's fight in 204 leaves Leon at 156 life (above), not the 60 the later warps gave him, so the rest is changed for Fine. A player in Fine turns 80 a tick where one in Caution turns 48, and walks and runs faster, so room 205's walk (17652 later) keeps `leon_a_205.txt`'s path with shorter turns and room 208's (19720 later) keeps `leon_a_208.txt`'s with shorter turns and a walk a tick shorter; both reach the same places, and the ribbons go into slot 3 on 20382. Room 207's zombies draw their lives from `Rnd` at the room's load, which the route carries in from the window licker: 40, 84, 70 and 84. At 156 life with those, every fight tried on arklay ended in a grab, so the route fires one round into room 205's corridor first (its four `Rnd` draws give 55, 56, 50 and 48 instead), and room 207's input is new (18433 later): the status screen's reload as `leon_a_207.txt`'s, then slot 3 from the north, slot 0 from a step back south, slot 2 after a turn to the corridor, slot 1 from the corridor's west end, four hits each with none grabbing, and the run east to door 2, which a short walk south reaches. The run matches on every one of its 20620 ticks, apart from `Rnd` and `rseed` on each door's tick, `msg`, `hk`, the music, the sounds, the enemies (`zm0` to `zm9`, `zp`) and the flags (`fgstat`, `fgitem`, `fgroom`) included, with a frame mean of 0.73 over all its frames, and each room past the card is judged on its own (`frame_windows`). It ignores room 100's opening on `zm1` (the load's tick) and `zp` (0 to 183, where the original draws slots 0 and 1 at 0x404040). `zd` is compared: arklay follows the memory a new work's `Dest` comes from (docs/internals/deviations/re2.md, "What a zombie's slot held before"), all but on the load's tick in room 100, room 101's slots 3 and 4 (memory from before the new game) until room 102's load, and room 103's slots 5 and 6 until room 200's load. The fix that got it there: a main whose `bgm_lut` row names no track still loads its `.BGM` bank and takes its word's hold (docs/internals/deviations/re2.md, "A music bank with no track"); room 201's licker scene writes main 9, `c449`.

Claire A past the card is checked the same way (`oracle/re2-regress.d/route_claire_a_208.toml`, #539): rooms 204, 205, 207 and 208 run Leon's scripts, and the warps are recorded with `--start claire` and compared with `player = 1` and `character = 1`. Each warp starts Claire as `route_claire_a_208` (below) brings her to that door. `claire_a_200_out` (`record --start claire --no-enemies --warp 200:-14847:-1800:1892:2136:6 --flag 4:6 --flag 4:7 --flag 4:102 --flag 4:70 --life 200 --give 0:3:7 --give 2:0x14:6`, `claire_a_200_out.txt`, 1300 ticks) starts where the computer hands Claire back, further south than Leon, with the handgun's 7 rounds and 6 spare. She turns east, runs round the upper floor to the counter and takes its 30 handgun rounds: area 19 holds them in scenario A for either character (`ck 1:1`, the grenade launcher with 6 is scenario B's), so Claire has 36 spare. Then the east ramp, the ground floor west and door 1, pressed on 1210, into 201 on 1245. It matches on every tick but the door's, with a frame mean of 0.09 over 970 to 1209, once the camera has left the upper floor's scrolled cut. `claire_a_204` (`record --start claire --warp 201:-15773:0:-21630:2040:0 --life 200 --give 0:3:7 --give 2:0x14:36`, `claire_a_204.txt`, 2500 ticks) is `leon_a_204.txt` with seven more presses of fire after the reload: Claire's handgun takes nine hits to kill the window licker (115 life, 14 a hit aimed down), and its 13-round magazine runs out after seven. The licker dies on 2209, and door 1, pressed on 2380, loads room 205 on 2415 with Claire at 104 life. Three runs compare it: `claire_a_201` to 594 (frame mean 0.19 over 380 to 502), `claire_a_204_entry` to 1700 (0.82, 0.60 with the message box blanked) and `claire_a_204` to 2500 (0.34 over 1700 to 2499); all three match on every tick but the doors' `Rnd` and `rseed`. `claire_a_205` (`--warp 205:-25670:0:-25775:3072:0 --life 119 --give 0:3:9 --give 2:0x14:38`, `claire_a_205.txt`, 900 ticks) fires one round into the corridor, then walks Leon's path at Fine's pace (`route_200_208.txt`'s) to door 2, pressed on 781; it matches on every tick, the head's from the warp included, with a frame mean of 0.33. Room 207 has three runs from the same warp (`--warp 207:-25640:0:-16590:0:1 --life 119 --give 0:3:8 --give 2:0x14:38`, with Leon's slot 2 `Dest`), whose zombies load with Leon's warp's lives, 65, 65, 118 and 55. In `claire_a_207` (`claire_a_207.txt`, 1580 ticks) slot 0, down as a crawler, takes hold of Claire's legs and she stamps on it: frame `0x1B` kicks its head off, and the head bounces on 967, 972 and 975, each bounce playing the zombie's sound 7 and drawing `Rnd` for its blood. arklay didn't send the head off (docs/internals/deviations/re2.md, "The kicked head"), so its stream fell a draw behind on 967. With it, the run matches on all its ticks: the others then take Claire down to 24 life, in both games, and she doesn't reach the door (frame mean 1.54, from the bodies close to cut 1's camera). `claire_a_207_door` (`claire_a_207_door.txt`, 1910 ticks) is a fight that puts all four down by 1500 with Claire at 104 life, and the run north and east along the corridor to door 2, pressed on 1785, into 208 on 1820. It matches on every tick but the door's, with a frame mean of 1.12 over 650 to 1784. `claire_a_207_grab` (`claire_a_207_grab.txt`, 1880 ticks) was the first try at it, designed in arklay before two fixes, and it is kept for them. Its START on 1085 comes while a zombie has hold of Claire: the original refuses it, since the hold keeps `fg_status`'s `0x40` up (and the aim before it did), where arklay had opened the status screen, so in the original she is bitten instead. Then, in the status screen opened on 1369, its run presses on 1644 and 1676 back out of the item grid as cancel presses do, since run and cancel are one button in the original, where arklay had only moved the cursor (docs/internals/deviations/re2.md, "START in a hold or the aim"). With both fixed it matches on every tick, with a frame mean of 1.01, and it doesn't reach the door. Room 208's two runs start from the route's state at its door (119 life, 6 rounds and 20 spare): `claire_a_208_entry` (`--no-enemies --warp 207:-15162:0:-13112:49:4`, 1450 ticks) turns to door 2 and opens it on 367, and `claire_a_208` (`--warp 208:-15462:0:-15336:1029:0`, 1100 ticks) starts on the arrival. Both walk Leon's Fine path to the ink ribbons (into slot 3), then west round the counter's end and south to the typewriter (area 2), whose prompt is paged and answered No (`claire_a_208`'s 1001). Both match on every tick, `tw` included, with a frame mean of 0.46. The typewriter's place was the first difference: a Claire A new game starts on place `0x0F`, as Leon A's does, where arklay had given her `0x10` (docs/internals/deviations/re2.md, "The typewriter").

`route_claire_a_208` (`oracle/re2-regress.d/route_claire_a_208.toml`, `route_claire_a_208.txt`, 21300 ticks, `--sound` and every frame, recorded in 84 minutes) is Claire's route from the new game to the darkroom's typewriter in one run, with no warp. Its first 14282 ticks are `route_claire_a_card.txt`'s, to the card's use, and play comes back on 14625. Each room's presses then follow from its door's hand back: `claire_a_200_out.txt`'s from 14282, door 1 pressed on 15492 into 201 on 15527; `claire_a_204.txt`'s from 15515, its fight started 12 ticks later than the warp's, door 1 pressed on 17907 into 205 on 17942 with Claire at 119 life; `claire_a_205.txt`'s from 17904, door 2 pressed on 18685 into 207 on 18720; a fight of its own in 207 from 18682, made again for the zombies this route brings (their lives are drawn from `Rnd` as the room loads), door 2 pressed on 20235 into 208 on 20270 with no hit taken; and `claire_a_208.txt`'s from 20232, the typewriter's prompt answered No on 21233. It matches on every tick but the doors' `Rnd` and `rseed` (`route_claire_a_card`'s ten, the last of them 4 ticks sooner in this recording, on 12932, and the five here) and room 100's opening `zp` to 183, as `route_200_208`'s. The frame mean is 0.74, and a window a room from each hand back: 200 (14625 to 15491) 0.53, 201 0.24, 204 0.48, 205 0.20, 207 1.19 (the bodies close to cut 1's camera) and 208 0.46.

Leon A to the S.T.A.R.S. office, from the darkroom (208) to the Unicorn Medal in room 115 (#582), is checked from two warps (`oracle/re2-regress.d/route_leon_a_115.toml`), each starting Leon as `route_200_208` leaves him: 156 life (Fine), 2 rounds in the handgun and 38 spare, the ink ribbons in slot 3, and `Rnd` 6114. `leon_a_208_114` (`record --warp 208:-19005:0:-16920:1029:0 --flag 8:141 --flag 6:71 --flag 6:72 --flag 6:115 --flag 6:116 --life 156 --give 0:2:2 --give 2:0x14:38 --give 3:0x1E:3`, `leon_a_208_114.txt`, 4150 ticks) starts where the ribbons' take leaves Leon. He walks round the counter's end to door 0 (pressed on 449, room 207 on 484, play back on 789), runs west past 207's dead zombies through the gap and walks to door 3, the stairs (940; 10C on 975). Up the stairs with action held, a turn on the spot and a running turn to exactly 2048, then `statues_10c.txt`'s puzzle 1092 ticks later (1090 from its second push, and its walk west a tick shorter), so both statues lock and the Red Jewel goes into slot 4. From the pedestal he runs west, then south along the hall to door 2 (3724; 114 on 3759, play back on 4066) and stops. It matches on every tick but the three doors' `Rnd` and `rseed`, with these left out: `om2` until 207's load (the original raises the warp's 8:141 before 208's init, so the taken ribbons' object is out of play there), the objects and `hk` during each load (the original lists the old room's objects until the new room's are set up) and 114's slot 0 `zm0` from 3850 (its `Spd`, what the slot held before). Its frame mean is 0.85; the rooms on their own are 0.11 (208), 0.16 (207), 0.54 (10C, the jewel's question and item view included) and 0.12 (114), and the stairs door is about 5.5 (docs/internals/deviations/re2.md, "The stairs door's frames"). Three fixes got it there: the jewel's twinkle follows its object and goes when it is taken (docs/internals/deviations/re2.md, "Attached effects"; before, its `Rnd` draws went on after the take, from 3656), a door's arrival tests its camera zones at the door's end ("The camera under a door"), and the load's `Sce_work_clr_at` clears the objects' areas ("The objects' areas at a load"). `leon_a_114_115` (`record --warp 114:-25260:0:-3420:2048:5 --life 156 --give 0:2:2 --give 2:0x14:38 --give 3:0x1E:3 --give 4:0x33:1`, `leon_a_114_115.txt`, 3250 ticks, voice lengths from the reference) starts on 114's arrival with the room's zombies on. Leon runs down the corridor past them, turns east and presses door 1 on 436 (115 on 472, play back on 778), walks east to the desk and presses it on 895: Chris's Diary opens on 932 and is paged and filed, the Unicorn Medal's question comes on 1342, Yes on 1615 puts it in slot 5 on 1635, and the press on 1815 starts the scene with Claire, which hands back about 3083. It matches on every tick but the door's `Rnd` and `rseed` and the head from 562 to 775 (Leon's head was turned toward a zombie as he pressed the door, docs/internals/deviations/re2.md, "The head through a door"). Its frame mean is 0.97: 114 is 0.38, 115 to the medal's question 0.21, and from there on 0.97, with the scene's props drawn on the hands and the scene itself about 1.07, Leon and Claire close to the camera ("Room 115's scene"). Frames 1342 to 1345 keep the desk's close-up as the screen fades, in both games (`cut_chg` with bit 7, docs/internals/deviations/re2.md, "The frames of a cut").

`route_200_115` (the same manifest, `route_200_115.txt`, 27250 ticks, `--sound` and every frame, recorded in about 110 minutes) carries `route_200_208` on to the scene's end in one run, with no warp. Its first 20619 ticks are `route_200_208.txt`'s; then `leon_a_208_114.txt`'s presses follow 20276 ticks later, so that its tick 343 falls on 208's hand back at 20619 (door 0 on 20725, the stairs door on 21216, 10C on 21251, door 2 on 24000, 114 on 24035), and `leon_a_114_115.txt`'s 23999 later (door 1 on 24435, 115 on 24470, the medal into slot 5 on 25634, the scene back about 27082). It matches on every tick but the doors' `Rnd` and `rseed` (`route_200_208`'s fifteen and the four here), room 100's opening as in `route_200_208`, the head through 114's door (as in the warp), and the objects outside room 10C: `om0` to `om2` are compared from 10C's load's end (21425) to door 2's load (24034), since elsewhere the original's slots out of play keep the last room's objects (docs/internals/deviations/re2.md, "What an object's slot held before"). The enemies (`zm0` to `zm9`, `zp`), the flags, `msg`, `hk`, `scene`, the music and the sounds all match. The frame mean is 0.78, and each room is judged from its hand back to its door's press: `route_200_208`'s six windows (208's ends at its door 0), then 207 again 0.16, 10C 0.54, 114 0.35, and 115 0.18 to the medal's close-up and 0.97 from there on, with the scene's props drawn on the hands ("Room 115's scene").

Claire A to the S.T.A.R.S. office, from the darkroom (208) to the Unicorn Medal and the exit scene in room 115 (#586), is checked from two warps (`oracle/re2-regress.d/route_claire_a_115.toml`), recorded with `--start claire` and compared with `player = 1` and `character = 1`, each starting Claire as `route_claire_a_208` leaves her: 119 life, 6 rounds in the handgun and 20 spare, the ink ribbons in slot 3, and `Rnd` 6114. Rooms 208, 207, 10C and 114 run Leon's scripts, and 115 runs Claire's own. `push_statue_10c_claire` (`record --start claire --no-enemies --warp 10C:-22905:-3600:-11900:1024 --life 200`, `push_statue_10c.txt`, 900 ticks) is `push_statue_10c` on Claire: her push, on her own clips 7 and 8, is about a tenth slower than Leon's, and her width (350) holds her 100 units further out of a statue. It matches on every tick, `pneck` included, with a frame mean of 0.01. It showed the head's one difference: `Om_move` holds the player out of an object after `Sca_ck_em`'s pushes, and the original's head follows the held-out body, where arklay's stayed on the step into the statue, up to a run's step ahead while Claire ran into it (docs/internals/deviations/re2.md, "The head in a push"). With that fixed, Leon's runs compare the head in the pushes too. `claire_a_208_114` (`record --start claire --warp 208:-19956:0:-19363:149:1 --flag 8:141 --flag 6:71 --flag 6:72 --flag 6:115 --flag 6:116 --life 119 --give 0:3:6 --give 2:0x14:20 --give 3:0x1E:3`, `claire_a_208_114.txt`, 4310 ticks) starts at the typewriter. Claire walks north along the table's west side to the wall at z -15462, east along the counter to the east wall at x -14346, and turns to door 0 (pressed on 535, room 207 on 570, play back on 875). From there `leon_a_208_114.txt`'s presses follow 86 ticks later through 207, door 3 (1026; 10C on 1061), the stairs and the run west. The statue puzzle is Leon's, with each approach and hold made again for Claire: the first push 4 ticks longer, the walk to statue 1's -x side a step shorter so that she stays on its face while the count runs (from 100 further out she would slide off it), and the pushes 20, 5, 24 and 15 ticks longer. The statues lock at (-17009, -12695) and (-25990, -12452), the jewel scene follows, and the Red Jewel goes into slot 4. Door 2 is pressed on 3878 (114 on 3913, play back on 4220). The run matches on every tick but the three doors' `Rnd` and `rseed`, with Leon's warp's object differences left out: `om2` until 207's load, the objects during each load, and object 0's floor a tick sooner as play comes back after the stairs door. Its frame mean is 0.82. The rooms on their own are 0.16 (208), 0.14 (207), 0.53 (10C) and 0.11 (114), and the stairs door is about 5.5, as on Leon. `claire_a_114_115` (`record --start claire --warp 114:-25260:0:-3420:2048:5 --life 119 --give 0:3:6 --give 2:0x14:20 --give 3:0x1E:3 --give 4:0x33:1`, `claire_a_114_115.txt`, 2850 ticks) starts on 114's arrival. The corridor is empty on Claire A until the Spade Key is taken (`main_00`'s `work_copy` into its `ck`: `ck(8, 93, 1)`), so `zm0` is `-` throughout. She runs Leon's path to door 1 (436; 115 on 471, play back on 777) and his presses to the desk. Event 5 (`main_05`) gives Chris's Diary through the file window, then the Unicorn Medal's question (Yes on 1615, slot 5 on 1635), with no scene. Then she goes west round the desk, north and east to the 15 handgun rounds (pressed on 2016, into slot 2's 20 on 2120), and west to the wall by door 0. With both pick-ups made, `main_01` has made door 0 event 3: its press on 2425 runs the exit scene (`main_03`, `main_04`): the music, cut 7, the ten `0x1116` effects, Claire's turn to 2768, objects 7 and 8 rising off the shelf one after the other, and 3:100 (`fgscn`). Play comes back on 2729. It matches on every tick but the door's `Rnd` and `rseed` and the objects during the load, with `esp`, `fl`, `fgscn` and `fgroom` (5:6, `main_01`'s latch) compared. Its frame mean is 0.71: 114 is 0.16, 115 to the desk's close-up 0.21, from its fade (1342 to 1345 keep the close-up in both games, "The frames of a cut") to door 0 0.67, and the exit scene 0.25.

`route_claire_a_115` (the same manifest, `route_claire_a_115.txt`, 27620 ticks, `--sound` and every frame, recorded in about two hours) carries `route_claire_a_208` on to the end of 115's exit scene in one run, with no warp. Its first 21237 ticks are `route_claire_a_208.txt`'s, to the typewriter's No. Then `claire_a_208_114.txt`'s presses follow 20891 ticks later, so that its tick 343 falls on 21234, where the reference closes the typewriter: door 0 on 21426, the stairs door on 21917 (10C on 21952), door 2 on 24769 (114 on 24804). `claire_a_114_115.txt`'s follow 24768 ticks later, from 114's hand back on 25111: door 1 on 25204 (115 on 25239), the medal into slot 5 on 26403, the rounds into slot 2 on 26888, door 0 on 27193 and the exit scene back on 27497. It matches on every tick but the doors' `Rnd` and `rseed` (`route_claire_a_208`'s fifteen and the four here), room 100's opening as in `route_claire_a_208`, and the objects outside rooms 10C and 115: `om0` to `om2` are compared from 10C's load's end (22045) to door 2's load, with object 0's floor a tick sooner after the stairs door (22125) left out, and `om3`, `om7`, `om8` and `om9` from 115's load's end (25330) on. The enemies (`zm0` to `zm9`, `zp`), the flags (`fgstat`, `fgitem`, `fgroom`, `fgcom`, `fgscn`), `msg`, `hk`, `tw`, `fl`, `scene`, the music and the sounds all match. The frame mean is 0.75. Each room is judged from its hand back to its door's press: `route_claire_a_208`'s five windows to 207, then 208 0.40 (from its door 2's hand back to door 0), 207 again 0.14, 10C 0.53, 114 0.15, and 115 0.18 to the desk's close-up, 0.67 from the medal's question (26110 to 26113 keep the close-up in both games) to door 0, and 0.25 over the exit scene.

Leon A to the Spade Key, from the S.T.A.R.S. office (115) back to the main hall's statue (200) (#593), is checked from three warps (`oracle/re2-regress.d/route_leon_a_spade.toml`), each starting Leon as `route_200_115` leaves him: 156 life, 2 rounds in the handgun and 38 spare, the ink ribbons in slot 3, the Red Jewel in slot 4, the Unicorn Medal in slot 5, `Rnd` 6114, every common (bank 4) and item (bank 8) flag the route has raised by its end, and the dead flags of room 207's four zombies (6:71, 6:72, 6:115, 6:116); room 114's three zombies and room 204's licker are alive. `leon_a_115_207` and `leon_a_201_spade` were recorded with 204's licker's dead flag (6:61) raised as well, which none of their rooms reads. `leon_a_115_207` (`--warp 115:-23037:0:-19397:2107:2`, `leon_a_115_207.txt`, 1960 ticks) starts where the scene with Claire hands back. Leon turns north, runs to the west wall and presses door 0 on 422 (114 on 457, play back on 763). Slot 0 stands by 114's door, so he turns on the spot to face north before he runs, up the corridor's east wall to its north end, turns east and presses door 2 on 864 (10C on 899, back on 1205). In 10C he turns north, runs to the upper floor's north side, turns east and runs along it past the two statues on their plates, turns south at the stairs' head, goes down with action held, turns west and presses door 0, the stairs door, on 1598 (207 on 1633, back on 1803). It matches on every tick but the three doors' `Rnd` and `rseed`, Claire (`em`) and the zombies included, once the setup gives room 114's slot 2 the `Dest` its work's memory holds, -32768, -32768 (docs/internals/deviations/re2.md, "What a zombie's slot held before"). Its frame mean is 1.24: the rooms on their own are 0.31 (115), 0.37 (114), 0.23 (10C) and 0.16 (207), and the stairs door is about 4.1 (docs/internals/deviations/re2.md, "The stairs door's frames"). `leon_a_207_201_licker` (`--warp 207:-23650:0:-8000:2048:2`, without 6:61, `leon_a_207_201_licker.txt`, 2000 ticks) runs west past 207's dead zombies and south down the corridor to door 1 (436; 205 on 471, back on 777). Room 10C's load has raised 4:48, so in 205 both groups of bodies come out of the walls. Leon runs north-west to the north corridor and west along it about 2100 from the east group's wall (event 4's area, slots 5 to 9), then south down the west corridor along its east wall (x -25253 to -25533) past the west group (event 3's area, slots 0 to 4), whose slot 4 starts its reach as he passes about 1660 from it, with no grab; door 0 on 1086 (204 on 1121, back on 1427). `route_200_208` leaves room 204's licker alive (17 life; 6:61 clear), and with 4:86 set `main_02` hangs a new one (101 life) from the corridor's ceiling at -16169, -6579. Leon turns a little east and runs down the corridor's east wall, so that it drops as he passes, too far off to reach him, then turns east at a run into the east corridor and presses door 0 on 1622 (201 on 1657, back on 1963) with it about 19800 behind. It matches on every tick but the three doors' `Rnd` and `rseed`, all ten bodies and the licker (`zm0` to `zm9`) included, with a frame mean of 0.76: 207 0.14, 205 0.34, 204 0.29 and 201 0.26. An earlier recording with 6:61 raised (`leon_a_207_201`, kept in the references) has no licker. `leon_a_201_spade` (`--warp 201:-26492:0:-25629:0:4`, `leon_a_201_spade.txt`, 1500 ticks) runs north and east to door 0 (470; 200 on 505, back on 837), then across the main hall onto the statue's south face, where it stops on 910 with its probe in area 13, and turns to face north. START on 926, the cursor down twice and right to slot 5, the menu on 1022 and USE on 1042: the screen closes on 1052 and event 8 starts on 1055 (cut 14, sound 2:d, film 5 off), and the medal leaves the inventory on 1140 (cut 8). A press on the statue on 1160 starts event 7 (cut 13); the Spade Key's question comes on 1197, Yes on 1300 puts it in slot 5 as `59:2` on 1320, and the close on 1380 brings cut 8 back on 1392. It matches on every tick but the door's `Rnd` and `rseed`, `movie`, `fguse`, `scv` and `atsce` included. Two fixes got it there: the status screen's close and the get-item window's run `St_exit`, which loads the room's view again on the tick `Cut_no` comes back (`movie` 1 there), and the get-item window's take writes the item to variable 3 (docs/internals/deviations/re2.md, "Using a key item"). Its frame mean is 1.36: 201 0.27, 200 0.10 to START, 0.73 through the status screen, 0.25 over event 8 and 0.58 over event 7 and the key's window; the door into the hall is about 3.3 (docs/internals/deviations/re2.md, "The main hall's door's frames").

`route_200_spade` (the same manifest, `route_200_spade.txt`, 31316 ticks, `--sound` and every frame, recorded in about 145 minutes) carries `route_200_115` on to the Spade Key in one run, with no warp. Its first 27081 ticks are `route_200_115.txt`'s; then each warp's presses follow so that its tick 343 falls on the route's tick before that room's door: `leon_a_115_207.txt`'s 26738 ticks later (115's door 0 on 27160, 114's door 2 on 27602, 10C's stairs door on 28336), `leon_a_207_201_licker.txt`'s 28197 later (207's door 1 on 28633, 205's door 0 on 29283, 204's door 0 on 29819) and `leon_a_201_spade.txt`'s 29816 later (201's door 0 on 30286, START on 30742, the medal used on 30858, the key taken on 31136). It matches on every tick but the doors' `Rnd` and `rseed` (`route_200_115`'s nineteen and the seven here), `route_200_115`'s other known differences, the objects outside 10C's first visit as there, `hk` over each room's load (the object list it carries differs as the objects do) and the view's load on the new game's first tick (`movie`). The enemies (`zm0` to `zm9`, `zp`), Claire (`em`), the flags, `fguse`, `scv`, `atsce`, `msg`, the music and the sounds all match, 204's new licker included. Room 114's slot 2 starts with the `Dest` its work's memory holds on each visit, 8481, 9766 on the way to the office and -32768, -32768 on the way back, as arklay's memory of the route has them (docs/internals/deviations/re2.md, "What a zombie's slot held before"). The frame mean is 0.83, and each room is judged from its hand back to its door's press: `route_200_115`'s windows (115's last running on to the hand back at 27081, 1.04), then 115 again 0.33, 114 0.39, 10C 0.23, 207 0.14, 205 0.34, 204 0.28, 201 0.27, and in the hall 0.10 to START, 0.73 through the status screen, 0.16 over event 8 and 0.58 over event 7 and the key's window.

Claire A to the Spade Key, from the S.T.A.R.S. office (115) back to the main hall's statue (200) (#610), is checked from three warps (`oracle/re2-regress.d/route_claire_a_spade.toml`), recorded with `--start claire` and compared with `player = 1` and `character = 1`, each starting Claire as `route_claire_a_115` leaves her: 119 life, 6 rounds in the handgun and 35 spare, the ink ribbons in slot 3, the Red Jewel in slot 4, the Unicorn Medal in slot 5, `Rnd` 6114, the exit scene's 3:100, every common (bank 4) and item (bank 8) flag the route has raised by its end, and the dead flags of room 204's window licker (6:61) and room 207's four zombies (6:71, 6:72, 6:115, 6:116). Rooms 114, 10C, 207, 205 and 204 run Leon's scripts; 115 is Claire's own, and 201's and 200's differ from Leon's only in two pick-ups off the route (201's area 9 and 200's area 19). Each warp's input is Leon's, and the original's timeline from each hand back is his to the tick; only the rooms differ. `claire_a_115_207` (`--warp 115:-26382:0:-18845:2768:2`, `claire_a_115_207.txt`, 1900 ticks) starts where the exit scene leaves Claire, by door 0: with 3:100 set, `main_00` keeps objects 7 and 8 off the shelf and `main_01` no longer makes the door event 3. She turns west and presses door 0 on 364 (114 on 399, play back on 705). Room 114 is empty on Claire A until the Spade Key is taken (`main_00`'s Claire branch reads `ck(8, 93, 1)`), so the corridor has none of Leon's three zombies; from there `leon_a_115_207.txt`'s presses follow 58 ticks sooner: door 2 on 806 (10C on 841, back on 1147) and the stairs door on 1540 (207 on 1575, back on 1745). It matches on every tick but the three doors' `Rnd` and `rseed`, with a frame mean of 1.26: the rooms on their own are 0.20 (115), 0.12 (114), 0.23 (10C) and 0.15 (207), and the stairs door is about 4.1, as on Leon. `claire_a_207_201` (`--warp 207:-23650:0:-8000:2048:2`, `claire_a_207_201.txt`, `leon_a_207_201_licker.txt`'s presses, 2000 ticks) runs past 207's dead zombies to door 1 (436; 205 on 471, back on 777), through the corridor of bodies with 4:48 set, both groups out of the walls and out of reach, to door 0 (1086; 204 on 1121, back on 1427). Claire killed 204's window licker on her way out (`claire_a_204`), so `main_02` hangs no new one from the ceiling, as in Leon's `leon_a_207_201` with 6:61 raised; she runs the corridor to door 0 (1622; 201 on 1657, back on 1963). It matches on every tick but the doors' `Rnd` and `rseed`, all ten bodies (`zm0` to `zm9`) included, with a frame mean of 0.74: 207 0.14, 205 0.33, 204 0.21 and 201 0.26. `claire_a_201_spade` (`--warp 201:-26492:0:-25629:0:4`, `claire_a_201_spade.txt`, `leon_a_201_spade.txt`'s presses, 1500 ticks) runs to door 0 (470; 200 on 505, back on 837), onto the statue's south face, and uses the Unicorn Medal from slot 5 as Leon does: START on 926, event 8 on 1055 (the medal gone on 1140), event 7 from the press on 1160, and the Spade Key into slot 5 as `59:2` on 1320. It matches on every tick but the door's `Rnd` and `rseed`, `movie`, `fguse`, `scv` and `atsce` included, with a frame mean of 1.36: 201 0.25, 200 0.09 to START, 0.72 through the status screen, 0.25 over event 8 and 0.57 over event 7 and the key's window; the door into the hall is about 3.3, as on Leon.

`route_claire_a_spade` (the same manifest, `route_claire_a_spade.txt`, 31673 ticks, `--sound` and every frame, recorded in about 2 hours 40 minutes) carries `route_claire_a_115` on to the Spade Key in one run, with no warp. Its first 27496 ticks are `route_claire_a_115.txt`'s, to the exit scene's hand back on 27497; then each warp's presses follow so that its tick 343 falls on the route's tick before that room's hand back: `claire_a_115_207.txt`'s 27153 ticks later (115's door 0 on 27517, 114's door 2 on 27959, 10C's stairs door on 28693), `claire_a_207_201.txt`'s 28554 later (207's door 1 on 28990, 205's door 0 on 29640, 204's door 0 on 30176) and `claire_a_201_spade.txt`'s 30173 later (201's door 0 on 30643, START on 31099, the medal gone on 31313, the key into slot 5 on 31493). It matches on every tick but the doors' `Rnd` and `rseed` (`route_claire_a_115`'s nineteen and the seven here), `route_claire_a_115`'s other known differences, the objects outside 10C's and 115's first visits as there, `hk` over each room's load, and the view's load on the new game's first tick (`movie`), as in `route_200_spade`. The enemies (`zm0` to `zm9`, `zp`), the flags (`fgscn` among them), `fguse`, `scv`, `atsce`, `msg`, the music and the sounds all match. This recording's original runs a tick apart from `route_claire_a_115`'s between the computer (12210) and the card's room (about 15400), as two recordings of the original can, and arklay's replay follows it: the card's door draws its number on 12933 here (12932 there) and its load runs to 13058. The frame mean is 0.81, and each room is judged from its hand back to its door's press: `route_claire_a_115`'s windows, the exit scene to its hand back 0.27, then 115 again 0.21, 114 0.12, 10C 0.33, 207 0.14, 205 0.33, 204 0.21, 201 0.26, and in the hall 0.09 to START, 0.72 through the status screen, 0.24 over event 8 and 0.57 over event 7 and the key's window. 10C's is 0.23 in the warp: here the original shows cut 6 a frame late as Claire runs into it after the stairs door (28332, a mean of 42.6 on its own), as a recording can after a slow tick, where its warp's recording and arklay show it when the hold ends (docs/internals/deviations/re2.md, "The frames of a cut").

Leon A to the Diamond Key, from the main hall's statue (200) through the Spade Key's two doors (204's door 2 into 203, 114's door 0 into 113) toward the Diamond Key in 10B (#615), is checked from warps (`oracle/re2-regress.d/route_leon_a_diamond.toml`), each starting Leon as the run before leaves him, the first as `route_200_spade` leaves him: 156 life, 2 rounds in the handgun and 38 spare, the ink ribbons in slot 3, the Red Jewel in slot 4, the Spade Key (`59:2`) in slot 5, every common (bank 4) and item (bank 8) flag `route_200_spade` has raised, both of its `fg_key` bits (31:17, 31:27, so `fgkey` compares from the first tick), and room 207's four zombies dead. Each setup gives `Rnd` 6114, where the original's warp leaves it before the room's init: arklay's `set_re2_rnd` runs the init again, so a room whose init draws (204's draws 8 times) reaches the reference's tick-343 value from there. `leon_a_200_203` (`--warp 200:-13862:0:-10012:3080:8`, `leon_a_200_203.txt`, 2600 ticks) turns from the statue toward door 1 and runs along the hall onto its wall (457; 201 on 492, play back on 825), runs `leon_a_201`'s walk through 201 to door 1 (949; 204 on 984, back on 1291). With 4:86 set and 6:61 clear, `main_02` hangs 204's licker from the corridor's ceiling, and it drops as play starts. Leon runs west along the corridor, turns toward the branch north and fires aimed down every 12 ticks: six hits kill it on 1595 (6:61), and its one scratch takes him to 134. He runs up the branch to door 2 and turns east onto it: the press on 1720 shows the used-key message with the key cue (`fg_key` bit 5) and arms `Key_lost` (`hk` `2,6`: the hook and the key's slot + 1), the close on 1780 takes a use off (`59:1`) and ends the hook, and the press on 1840 opens the door (203 on 1875, back on 2177). 203's Crank and rounds are behind its Id 2 box, whose push isn't ported, so Leon turns about and presses door 0 (2208; 204 on 2243, back on 2550). `leon_a_204_10c` (`--warp 204:-14423:0:-7289:2024:4`, with 6:61 and 31:5, 134 life, 14 rounds in the handgun and 20 spare, `leon_a_204_10c.txt`, 1750 ticks) runs north up the corridor to door 1 (420; 205 on 455, back on 762), through the corridor of bodies with 4:48 set, north along the west corridor's east wall, east along the north corridor and south to door 2, where neither group leaves the walls (1060; 207 on 1095, back on 1402), and through 207 past its dead zombies to the stairs door (1490; 10C on 1525, back on 1700). `leon_a_10c_113` (`--warp 10C:7390:0:944:960:0`, `leon_a_10c_113.txt`, 3180 ticks) goes up the stairs with action held, west along the upper floor past the statues on their plates and south to door 2 (749; 114 on 784, back on 1091). Leon turns south at the corridor's north end and fires every 6 ticks at the nearest standing zombie within 7000 as 114's three come up the corridor one at a time: slots 1 and 0 die (6:81, 6:78), slot 2 is left with 1 life and walks off north, and none reaches him. He runs down the corridor's east wall and east to door 0, where the press on 2646 shows the used-key message (`fg_key` bit 9), the close on 2706 takes the Spade Key's last use (`59:0`) and asks the discard question, Yes on 2766 empties the slot, and the press on 2826 opens the door (113 on 2861, back on 3168). All three match on every tick but the doors' `Rnd` and `rseed` and `hk` over each load, the licker (`zm1`), the corpse (`zm0`), the ten bodies and 114's zombies (`zm0` to `zm9`) included. Their frame means are 1.08, 1.37 and 0.63, from the doors: each room on its own is 0.11 to 0.30, and 114's fight 0.62. `leon_a_113_10a` (`--warp 113:-26100:0:-24800:0:0`, with 6:61, 6:78, 6:81, 31:5 and 31:9, 134 life, 16 rounds in the handgun and 2 spare, `leon_a_113_10a.txt`, 2520 ticks) runs north and east through 113 as event 6 wakes its zombies, and none reaches him (door 1 on 502; 112 on 537, back on 842), along the library's ground floor to door 0 (939; 110 on 974, back on 1307), round 110's corridors past its three zombies to door 0 (1747; 10F on 1782, back on 2099), and through 10F to door 0 (2165; 10A on 2200, back on 2507). It matches on every tick but the doors' `Rnd` and `rseed` and `hk` over each load, 113's and 110's zombies (`zm0` to `zm4`) included. Its frame mean is 1.28, and each room on its own is 0.12 to 0.87. 113's is the highest: on 453 to 457 a zombie lies right under the camera, which arklay drew as only an arm (1.31 and 1.26 then) until models were clipped at the original's near plane (`docs/internals/deviations/re2.md`, "Models at the near plane"). What's left there is the texture's filtering. Room 10A's fire (4:0 clear) blocks door 2: `Sce_Damage` (`0x4E9B70`) pushes the player away from an area's centre at 200 on each entry, and door 2's frame is reached only through areas 9 and 10. A recording of the original walking into it with `--no-enemies` matches arklay's push tick for tick, so the route goes to 108's water tank (4:0) first. `leon_a_10a_106b` (`--warp 10A:-25854:0:-19443:4016:1`, with 4:129 too, `leon_a_10a_106b.txt`, 2590 ticks) takes the way round: 10A's door 0 (418; 109 on 453, back on 758), east and north through 109 past its six crows and west to door 0 (1078; 108 on 1113, back on 1420), and north and east through 108 to area 11, whose event 13 opens door 0 (`aot_on`) down the stairs into 107 (1553; 107 on 1589). Leon runs south and east round 107's 0x13 (slot 3), which gets one hold on him (134 to 84), up the east corridor and west along the north side past slots 1 and 0 to door 0 (2235; 106 on 2270, back on 2572). It matches on every tick but the doors' `Rnd` and `rseed` (on the tick after the area's press for the stairs) and `hk` over each load, the crows and 107's zombies included. Three engine fixes came with it. On the tick a door is taken, `Sce_Door` has raised `fg_stop`'s `0xFF000000` before `Esp_move`, so only the kinds that carry on under a stop move: on 109's door the crows' feathers draw `Rnd` once, not four times. A door the scripts open stops the characters on that tick only, and the next room's zombies run their first routine in the load's last pass (107's set their life there, 91 ticks into the stairs). And `flr_set` (`0x4E8BE0`) turns a floor zone on or off: 109's events turn on its puddle (zone 1, sound `0x1a`), which Leon's run crosses. Its frame mean is 2.20, most of it the stairs (door 14), which the original draws about three times darker; each room on its own is 0.10 to 0.76. `leon_a_106_108` (`--warp 106:-17416:0:-559:1888:1`, with 4:3, 4:10 and 4:49 too, 84 life, `leon_a_106_108.txt`, 3780 ticks) starts on 106's arrival. Leon runs west and north up the aisle to the valve handle (item 50: Yes on 660, slot 5 on 680) and west to the 15 handgun rounds (Yes on 930, 17 in slot 2 on 950), then back to door 1 (1258; 107 on 1293, back on 1600). In 107 he runs east along the north side and shoots slot 1 at the corner and slots 2 and 3 down the east corridor (the handgun reloads itself when empty, on 2223); slot 3 hits him twice (84 to 74), and slot 0 is left standing. Door 1 (2562; 108 on 2597, back on 2772) leads to 108. He runs west and south to the water tank's area 3, opens the status screen, and uses the valve handle. Event 6 runs the scene that puts out the fire, raises 4:0 on 3678, and ends with event 7's `aot_on 4`, an `Sce_Status` area, which opens the status screen by itself on the next tick (3699). The run matches on every tick except the doors' `Rnd` and `rseed`, `hk` over each load, the head on the warp's first ticks (344 to 351), and one sound, a shell case's bounce on 2064. The case's place comes from the shot's effects, which arklay doesn't yet put where the original does (the flash doesn't follow the hand, and the blood starts about 150 units away): `snd` gives pan 10 there where the original gives 9 (#624). The effects pool (`esp`) isn't compared. Two engine fixes came with it. `Sce_Status` (kind 12) is ported. `Snd_bgm_ck` fades a sub sequence only while its channel plays, so 107's sub 5, whose second channel has no track, keeps that sequence's state through the door into 108 (`docs/internals/deviations/re2.md`, "A door to a new sub"). Its frame mean is 0.96: 106 is 0.46, 107 0.28, and 108 with the scene and the status screen 1.13. `leon_a_108_10b` (`--warp 108:-19746:0:-21336:2048:15`, with 4:0 and the 107 and 109 flags the run before raised, 74 life, `leon_a_108_10b.txt`, 2600 ticks) starts at the water tank. Leon runs north and east to door 1 (480; 109 on 515, back on 821), round 109's east side past its five crows and south to door 0 (1179; 10A on 1214, back on 1520). 10A's fire is out: with 4:0 set, `main_00` turns the fire's collision off (`sca_id_set(6, 0)`) and cut 0's zone to cut 6 on. Leon runs north, fires every 8 ticks at slot 0 as it comes (ten rounds, 13 to 3), and runs north and east along the corridor past slot 1 to door 2 (1971; 10B on 2006, back on 2312). In 10B he runs south-west to the shelf and takes the Diamond Key (Yes on 2475, slot 5 on 2495, two uses). It matches on every tick except the doors' `Rnd` and `rseed`, `hk` over each load, and the head on the warp's first ticks. One engine fix came with it. The original's `Sca_id_set` and `Cut_be_set` write the room as the scripts run, and arklay applied what a pass queued only after a scheduler pass. After a door that pass waits for play, so 109's record 21, which its `init_00` turns off, still pushed Leon 860 east on the door's last stopped tick, and 10A's door end tested its zones without cut 0's zone 2 (cut 6 came two ticks late). A room's load now applies what its own passes changed before anything moves. Its frame mean is 0.80: 108 is 0.28, 109 0.18, 10A 1.13 and 10B 0.55. On 1891 the original's capture repeats the frame before as 10A's cut 9 comes up (34.6).

Claire A to the Diamond Key, from the main hall's statue (200) through the Spade Key's two doors (204's door 2 into 203, 114's door 0 into 113) toward the Diamond Key in 10B (#665), is checked from warps (`oracle/re2-regress.d/route_claire_a_diamond.toml`), recorded with `--start claire` and compared with `player = 1` and `character = 1`, each starting Claire as the run before leaves her, the first as `route_claire_a_spade` leaves her: 119 life, 6 rounds in the handgun and 35 spare, the knife, the ink ribbons in slot 3, the Red Jewel in slot 4, the Spade Key (`59:2`) in slot 5, 3:100, every common (bank 4) and item (bank 8) flag `route_claire_a_spade` has raised, its two `fg_key` bits (31:17, 31:27), the window licker's dead flag (6:61) and room 207's four zombies dead, with `Rnd` 6114. Rooms 200, 201, 204, 205, 207 and 10C run Leon's scripts; Claire runs about 56 a tick in a running turn where Leon runs 48, so Leon's presses are made again where a path turns. `claire_a_200_203` (`--warp 200:-13846:0:-10035:3080:8`, `claire_a_200_203.txt`, 2331 ticks) turns from the statue to door 1 and runs along the hall onto its wall (457; 201 on 492, back on 825), takes `leon_a_201`'s walk through 201 to door 1 (949; 204 on 984, back on 1291), and with 6:61 set finds no licker in 204: she runs west along the corridor and up the branch to door 2, where the press on 1451 shows the used-key message (`fg_key` bit 5), the close on 1511 takes a use off (`59:1`) and the press on 1571 opens the door (203 on 1606, back on 1908). She turns about and presses door 0 (1939; 204 on 1974, back on 2281). `claire_a_204_10c` (`--warp 204:-14423:0:-7289:2024:4`, with the Spade Key at `59:1`, `claire_a_204_10c.txt`, 1750 ticks) is `leon_a_204_10c`'s presses: door 1 (420; 205 on 455, back on 762), the corridor of bodies with 4:48 set, door 2 (1060; 207 on 1095, back on 1402) and the stairs door (1490; 10C on 1525, back on 1700). `claire_a_10c_113` (`--warp 10C:7390:0:944:960:0`, `claire_a_10c_113.txt`, 2130 ticks) goes up the stairs and west along the upper floor to door 2 (749; 114 on 784). With 8:93 set and 4:40 clear, Claire's branch of 114's `main_00` places Sherry (0x45) and one zombie (slot 0, id 78), raises 4:40 and starts event 2 at the load (875), behind the door, which plays on until 1088. Sherry runs from the zombie to door 0 and leaves (`plc_dest`, `xa_on` 41 and 42, `cut_chg`, the music table), and play comes back on 1089. Its voice lines end by the recording's clock, so the run takes their length from the reference (`voice_from_ref`). Claire aims from 1330 and fires every 8 ticks at the zombie as it comes up the corridor until it dies, runs down the corridor to door 0, and uses the Spade Key's last use there: the used-key message on 1873 (`fg_key` bit 9), the close on 1933 (`59:0` and the discard question), Yes on 1993, and the press on 2053 that opens the door (113 on 2088). All three match on every tick but the doors' `Rnd` and `rseed` and `hk` over each load; 205's bodies and 114's zombie (`zm0` to `zm9`) are included. `claire_a_10c_113` also leaves out the head (`pneck`) while 114's load runs its scene behind the door (docs/internals/deviations/re2.md, "The head through a door") and the zombie's speeds until its first move ("What a zombie's slot held before"). It needed one engine fix: a zombie's rise again (`FUN_00455170`, which the scene's `main_05` starts as it lets the zombie go), its landing from a throw and its fall toward the player keep the stagger counter they draw (`At_sce_no`) and clear `At_obj_no`, so the third hit staggers it as in the original rather than knocking it back. Their frame means are 1.49, 1.39 and 0.35. Each worst frame is the original's capture repeating the frame before on a door's tick or a cut (34.4 on 200_203's 1572, 29.0 on 204_10c's 999); the doors' own frames are 5 to 12.

`claire_a_114_10a` (`--warp 114:-14296:0:-26349:4086:0`, with 4:40, 4:255, 6:78, 31:5 and 31:9 too, 9 rounds in the handgun and 22 spare and the Spade Key gone, `claire_a_114_10a.txt`, 3793 ticks) starts by 114's door 0, its lock open, rather than in 113: 113's first-entry scene runs at the load, so a warp straight into the room would play it before the warp's tick 344. The press on 350 opens the door (113 on 385). With 4:15 clear, Claire's `main_00` places a script character (0x50), raises 4:15 and starts event 2 at the load, a camera scene with `cut_chg` and `cut_auto`, and play comes back on 1667. 113 has no zombies on Claire A. Claire runs north and east along the north strip to door 1 (1775; 112 on 1810, back on 2115), along the library's ground floor east of the floor that gives way to door 0 (2212; 110 on 2247, back on 2580), round 110's corridors past its three zombies to door 0 (3020; 10F on 3055, back on 3372), and through 10F to door 0 (3438; 10A on 3473, back on 3780). From 112 on the presses are `leon_a_113_10a`'s, 1273 ticks later. It matches on every tick but the doors' `Rnd` and `rseed` and `hk` over each load, 110's zombies included. Their speeds until they first move are what the original's memory held there, which the setup script gives (`api:set_re2_enemy_speed`; docs/internals/deviations/re2.md, "What a zombie's slot held before"). Its frame mean is 1.11, and its worst frames are the original's capture repeating the frame before.

The extinguish detour plays as on Leon's route. `claire_a_10a_106` (`--warp 10A:-25854:0:-19443:4016:1`, with 4:15 too, `claire_a_10a_106.txt`, 2445 ticks) starts on 10F's door's arrival with 10A on fire and takes `leon_a_10a_106b`'s presses: 10A's door 0 (418; 109 on 453, back on 758), 109 past its crows to door 0 (1078; 108 on 1113, back on 1420), and 108's area 11, whose event opens the stairs into 107 (1554; 107 on 1589). In 107, Leon's loop round the 0x13, which ended in its hold, is a straight run for Claire, whom it doesn't reach: up the east corridor and west along the north side to door 0 (2085; 106 on 2120, back on 2427). `claire_a_106_108` (`--warp 106:-17416:0:-559:1888:1`, with 4:3, 4:10 and 4:49 too, `claire_a_106_108.txt`, 3019 ticks) is made for Claire. 106's valve handle (item 50, 8:8) lies at area 4, and the bowgun (item 12) on the next shelf, where Leon's rounds lie, is left. She runs west and north up the aisle and takes the valve handle (slot 6 on 549), back to door 1 (740; 107 on 775, back on 1082), and through 107 shooting slots 1, 2 and 3 with aimed and aimed-down shots (the handgun reloads 13 from the spare 22 on 1480; slot 0 is left), to door 1 (1785; 108 on 1820). In 108 she runs west and south to the water tank's area 3 and steps on into the USE's reach. The status screen's USE of the valve handle runs event 6, whose scene raises 4:0 on 2921. Both match on every tick but the doors' `Rnd` and `rseed` and `hk` over each load, 109's crows and 106's and 107's zombies included. The zombies' speeds until they first move are given by the setup scripts, as for 110. Their frame means are 2.33 and 1.13. The first is mostly 108's stairs door, which the original draws darker (#676, as on Leon's route).

`claire_a_108_10b` (`--warp 108:-19920:0:-21250:2048:0`, with 4:0, 6:19, 6:207, 6:208 and 8:8 too and the valve handle in slot 6, `claire_a_108_10b.txt`, 2420 ticks) starts at the water tank. Claire runs Leon's path north and east to door 1 (480; 109 on 515), with the turn made again for her: release on 453, right from 455 to 471. She goes round 109's east side past its crows to door 0 (1179; 10A on 1214). With the fire out (4:0), Claire's 10A has no zombies (`main_00`'s `ck(1, 0, 0)` places them for Leon only). Walking east along the corridor into area 6 (`-17888, -9080`, 1800 by 2200) runs her event 2: 3:32, a voice line (`xa_on` 32) and `main_03`'s `plc_neck`, `plc_motion` and `plc_dest`, handing back on 1724. She runs on to door 2 (1784; 10B on 1819), and in 10B, on Leon's presses 187 ticks earlier, to the shelf and the Diamond Key (`5a:2` in slot 7 on 2308). It matches on every tick but the doors' `Rnd` and `rseed` and `hk` over each load, 109's crows included. One engine fix came with it. `Pl_init` gives Claire's bodies and Sherry (ids 1, 3, 5, 7, 9 and 0xE) a box height (`Ofs_y` and `At_h`) of 0x546, and id 0xF 0x32A, over `Player_set`'s 0x5FA. arklay gave every player Leon's 0x5FA, so on 934 a crow flying low pushed Claire out of its box, where the original's box passes under it. The same height lets `licker_204_fight_claire` match (docs/internals/deviations/re2.md, "What a climbing licker doesn't do"). Its frame mean is 0.64, and its worst is 109's door load (8.3).

`route_200_diamond` (`oracle/re2-regress.d/route_leon_a_diamond.toml`, `route_200_diamond.txt`, 47946 ticks, `--sound` and every frame, recorded in about five hours) is Leon A's route from the new game to the Diamond Key in one run, with no warp. Its first 31316 ticks are `route_200_spade.txt`'s, and from the statue each warp above goes on from the route's hand back after the door before: a warp's tick 344 falls on that hand back. The route's `Rnd` isn't the warps' 6114, so its enemies draw other lives and choices, and the fights are made again. 204's licker has 101 life (`Em22_Life[Rnd & 0xF]`). After a wait of 40 ticks at 204's hand back, eight hits kill it and its one scratch takes Leon to 134, and the Spade Key's use at door 2 comes 69 ticks later than in the warp. That leaves 12 rounds in the handgun and 20 spare (204 back from 203 on 33590). `leon_a_204_10c`'s presses run as they are (10C back on 34945), and `leon_a_10c_113`'s up to 114's hand back (35691). 114's three zombies have 70, 84 and 65 life: Leon faces south and fires every 6 ticks at the nearest standing zombie within 6000 with more than 1 life, which leaves slot 1 with 1 life, and goes down the east wall to door 0 and the Spade Key's last use (113 back on 37891). `leon_a_113_10a`'s presses come a tick later than in the warp (10A back on 40054). `leon_a_10a_106b`'s wait 8 ticks at 109's hand back for its crows and 10 at 107's, where 0x13 holds Leon once (134 to 84; 106 back on 42300). From 106 to the end the input is made in one piece. 106's valve handle and 15 rounds come as in `leon_a_106_108`. 107 is made again for the route's zombies: Leon fires every 12 ticks at the nearest standing zombie within 6000, slot 1 at the corner and slots 2 and 3 down the east corridor, and none touches him. Then come 108's water tank and the valve handle's scene, START to close the status screen that event 7 opens, waypoints north and east to 108's door 1, and `leon_a_108_10b`'s presses from there, with a wait of 6 ticks at 10A's hand back. Leon takes the Diamond Key on 47791 with 84 life and 6 rounds. The run matches on every one of its 47946 ticks except route_200_spade's differences before the statue. Past the statue it differs only in `Rnd` and `rseed` on each door's tick, `hk` over each load, and the objects outside 10C (`om0` to `om2`, compared in 10C only, as `route_200_spade` does). The enemies (`zm0` to `zm9`, `zp`, `zd`), the flags (`fgstat`, `fgitem`, `fgroom`, `fgcom`, `fgkey`), the sounds and the music all match. 10A's burning zombie (slot 1 on the first visit) draws its parts in `FUN_00456780`'s colours, as the original does. Its frame mean is 0.95 over all its frames, and each room past the statue is judged on its own (`frame_windows`): 0.11 to 0.87, and 1.05 for 108 with the scene and the status screen. One engine fix came with it. When the status screen hands the room back, game mode 5 leaves `fg_stop` at `0xEF000000`, input bit included, for the next tick's `Pad_set`, as the get-item window's close and a door do. A direction held through the close moves the player a tick later, so after 108's status screen Leon turns a tick later than arklay had him, and the input made on arklay missed door 1 in the original (#636).

Marvin's turn (`oracle/re2-regress.d/marvin_turn_202.toml`): `marvin_turn_202` (`--warp 202:-23193:0:-9734:912:3`, the leg's flags, 64 life, `marvin_turn_202.txt`, 1200 ticks) plays `leon_a_209_heart`'s presses from 202's hand back: event 3, Marvin's turn into a zombie, his grab and bites and the Heart Key. Besides the player, the enemies and the flags it compares the oracle's `rc` field, each enemy slot's `Root_ck` turn (`+0x150`): Marvin, a scene character in slot 0, counts it down every tick (6 to 0 and 6 again), and when the script makes his work a zombie's (`member_set(6, 30)`) the zombie goes on from it. A probe through 209's door 7 (with the other enemies off) showed that the turn is 0 through a door's load until its last tick: `Sce_em_set` starts it at 2 (slot 0's number plus 2), and `Em_init_move`'s two `_Spl` calls (Marvin has a start) each count it once. Before arklay counted it, the zombie's turn started again at 2, and in `route_200_heart` Marvin looked for his way on other ticks once Leon had left his guide area (docs/internals/deviations/re2.md, "A scene character's `Root_ck` turn"). It also compares his parts (`zp`): with his arms swapped by the fade, the spares and the gun hand are off and the spares black ("Marvin's arms"). It matches on every tick, with a frame mean of 0.94.

Leon A to the Heart Key, from the Diamond Key in 10B down 110's ladder and through 207's Diamond door and 209 to Marvin's office (202) (#644), is checked from warps (`oracle/re2-regress.d/route_leon_a_heart.toml`), each starting Leon as the run before leaves him, the first as `route_200_diamond` leaves him: 84 life, 6 rounds in the handgun and none spare, the knife, the ink ribbons in slot 2, the Red Jewel in slot 3, the valve handle in slot 4, the Diamond Key (`5a:2`) in slot 5, every common (bank 4), item (bank 8) and `fg_key` (bank 31) flag `route_200_diamond` has raised, and the enemies' dead flags of the rooms before (bank 6). The Heart Key (0x5B, 8:113) lies in 202, whose door from the hall (200's door 2) is locked from inside once Marvin's scene has played (4:7), so the way in is 207's door 4, which the Diamond Key opens, through 209 and its door 7, which unlocks from 209's side. `leon_a_10b_200` (`--warp 10B:-19749:0:-25562:31:3`, `leon_a_10b_200.txt`, 2800 ticks) turns from 10B's shelf and runs north to door 1 (457; 10A on 493, back on 798), west along 10A's north corridor and south down its west one past its two zombies, alive again since nothing killed them, to door 1 (984; 10F on 1019, back on 1324), across 10F to door 1 (1401; 110 on 1436, back on 1753), and up 110's east corridor and west along its north one to the ladder, where it walks south onto area 1. The press on 2061 asks whether to lower the ladder; a press every 12 ticks finishes the text and answers Yes, and `main_02`'s event lowers it: the camera's cut 14, `se_on` 266 and 267, the vibration, the ladder's three objects moved down 40 a tick by `member_set2`, door 2 put back with `aot_reset`, and 4:148 raised. The event holds the enemies, so slot 2, which comes along the north corridor, stays about 2600 away. Once it ends (2355) the ladder's area asks again, now whether to go down (`main_02`'s branch with 4:148 set): presses on 2358, 2370 and 2382 finish the text and answer Yes, and `aot_on(2)` takes the ladder's door (200 on 2418, back on 2755) to the hall's upper floor by the ladder's foot. It matches on every tick but the doors' `Rnd` and `rseed` (the ladder's on the tick after its Yes), `hk` over each load and the warp's head on its first ticks, 10A's and 110's zombies (`zm0` to `zm2`) included. Its frame mean is 0.96: 10B 0.13, 10A 0.58, 10F 0.18, 110 0.14 through the ladder's event, and 200 from its hand back 1.01. `leon_a_200_209` (`--warp 200:-13956:-1800:6456:1024:5`, with 4:148 too, `leon_a_200_209.txt`, 5150 ticks) runs west along the hall's upper floor from the ladder's foot and down its west stairs to door 1 (575; 201 on 610, back on 943), through 201 to door 1 (1051; 204 on 1086, back on 1393), and along 204's corridor and up its branch to door 2, whose Spade Key lock is open (1688; 203 on 1723, back on 2030). With no rounds spare, the route takes 203's 15 (8:34), which #615 left behind the box: Leon runs round the shelf's north end, walks into the box's +z face from 2136 and pushes it south to the shelf, then goes round its west side and takes the rounds from there (Yes on 2594, slot 6 on 2606). Door 0 takes him back to 204 (2872; 204 on 2907, back on 3214), north to door 1 (3306; 205 on 3341, back on 3648), through the corridor of bodies with 4:48 set, out of both groups' reach, to door 2 (4188; 207 on 4223, back on 4530), and down 207's west wall to door 4. The press on 4657 shows the used-key message with the key cue (`fg_key` bit 8), the close on 4717 takes a use off (`5a:1`), and the press on 4777 opens the door (209 on 4812, back on 5114). It matches on every tick but the doors' `Rnd` and `rseed`, `hk` over each load and the warp's head, 205's bodies (`zm0` to `zm9`) and 203's box (`om0`, compared in 203 only, as `route_200_spade` compares 10C's objects) included. Its frame mean is 1.04: 200 0.32, 201 0.29, 204 0.31 and 0.22, 203 0.49 through the push and the rounds' window, 205 0.38, 207 0.18 and 209's first ticks 1.97. `leon_a_209_heart` (`--warp 209:-16934:0:-26037:2125:1`, with 8:34, 31:8 and 4:148 too and 203's rounds in slot 6, `leon_a_209_heart.txt`, 2450 ticks) fights 209's six zombies from the Diamond door's arrival: Leon stands through the warp's head and aims from 365, firing every 6 ticks at the nearest standing zombie within 4500. The first warp run, `leon_a_209_202`, aimed on 345, and from 351 its position was 3 off the original's: aiming beside the wall pushes Leon back by his hand's place, and the original's joint matrices are still stale from the warp on those ticks (the `pneck` the warp's head ignores), so the run was made again with the first aim after them. The handgun's 6 rounds go by 499, it reloads 203's 15 by itself (545), a zombie's hit takes Leon to 64 life on 601, and the last round goes on 1051, with slots 0 to 2 dead, slot 3 on 49 life and slots 4 and 5 not reached. Leon runs west along the south aisle and turns south at its west end to door 7, which is locked from 202's side: the press on 1167 unlocks it (`fg_key` bit 3) and the one on 1231 opens it (202 on 1266, back on 1573).
